name-double-dash-report {2026-10-26}
"X--of--Y" counts as one "--" between X and Y
names (<br>* Mcsh<Lang>.NAME, up to the first ! or ,) with "--"; "--" separates parts that have dashes themselves
scanned 254 Mcsh*.last.html; a name is listed once, under its worst "--"
total 20489

  NONE-DASH      953  no dash on either side of "--" (a--b): "--" not needed

  TRIPLE-DASH    196  "---" in the name
  EDGE           41  "--" at the start/end of a part or next to ' . / ( ) ('attribute--name-notation)

  BOTH-DASH      2191  both parts have dashes (syntax.subject-other--verb-argument): ok
